21 [Articles | InfoStealers] First Physical Robbery Potentially Linked to Revolut Data Breach First Physical Robbery Potentially Linked to Revolut Data Breach…
First Physical Robbery Potentially Linked to Revolut Data Breach Following the recent Revolut hack we reported on, we are now seeing the terrifying real-world consequences of compromised customer data. We are tracking what appears to be the first physical robbery potentially linked directly to the data breach. The trend of physical robberies stemming from cyber incidents is becoming more and more common, and is deeply concerning. The U.K. Home Invasion On October 13th, a cryptocurrency investor in the U.K. reported a horrific home invasion on X (formerly Twitter). The victim stated they were held at knifepoint, taped up, and violently robbed of approximately $800,000 in cryptocurrency, alongside physical valuables. Source: https://x.com/rrealist/status/2109813738559693002 The Revolut Connection What makes this incident particularly alarming in the context of our recent reporting is the victim&#8217;s banking affiliation. In a follow-up reply, when asked directly if they banked with Revolut, the victim confirmed that they did. The victim confirming their status as a Revolut customer, establishing a potential link to the recent data exposure. Given the breadth of the recent Revolut breach where hackers used compromised government emails to socially engineer the bank into handing over highly sensitive customer dossiers, threat actors now have access to exact physical addresses alongside financial profiles. This creates a highly targeted hit list for physical extortion and robbery. Correspondence demonstrating the hacker utilizing an Italian government email address to communicate with Revolut&#8217;s legal department. How This Could Have Been Prevented This escalating physical danger highlights the critical importance of proactive cybercrime intelligence. For the Revolut hack specifically, monitoring if official government emails that request customer data are not compromised in Infostealer logs would have likely prevented this entirely. As we reported back in September, attackers utilized compromised &#8216;pec.interno.it&#8217; (Italian Ministry of the Interior) credentials which were already visible in Hudson Rock&#8217;s cybercrime database to bypass Revolut&#8217;s verification processes. Read our initial reporting on the breach mechanics here: https://www.hudsonrock.com/blog/revolut-hackers-used-infostealers-for-elaborate-social-engineering Hudson Rock&#8217;s Cybercrime Intelligence database revealing numerous compromised credentials for the &#8216;interno.it&#8217; domain, providing an easy avenue for the attackers. 22 [Articles | InfoStealers] Hudson Rock Partners with TRM Labs as a Launch Data Partner for Ardence, the New AI Investigation Platform Partnership Announcement October 14, 2026 &middot; Cybercrime In…
Partnership Announcement October 14, 2026 &middot; Cybercrime Intelligence Hudson Rock Partners with TRM Labs as a Launch Data Partner for Ardence, the New AI Investigation Platform Bridging the gap between blockchain activity, technical attack infrastructure, and real-world cybercriminal identity to help government investigators, cyber threat analysts, and compliance teams disrupt modern AI-enabled crime. Hudson Rock is proud to be a data partner of Ardence, TRM Labs&#8217; new AI investigation platform. Learn more: trmlink.info/3UgApIh As threat actors increasingly weaponize artificial intelligence and infostealer malware to scale cyberattacks, ransomware operations, and illicit financial schemes, defenders and investigators face an unprecedented operational challenge. Today, Hudson Rock is thrilled to announce our official partnership with TRM Labs as a launch data partner within the Ardence Data Network , powering TRM Labs&#8217; newly unveiled AI investigation platform, Ardence . Built specifically for government investigators, cyber threat analysts, and compliance teams across more than 600 agencies and institutions in 75 countries, Ardence brings specialized intelligence from more than 60 partners into a single, AI-native platform to tackle cybercrime, fraud, money laundering, sanctions evasion, and terrorism financing. Hudson Rock joins the Ardence Data Network to enrich cybercrime and blockchain investigations with real-time infostealer and cybercriminal identity intelligence. &#8220;To catch sophisticated threat actors, investigators must bridge the gap between blockchain activity and cybercriminal identity. Partnering with TRM arms analysts with real-time visibility into high-profile cybercrime networks, drastically accelerating complex investigations from start to finish.&#8221; Alon Gal, CTO and Co-Founder, Hudson Rock Closing the Capacity Gap in the Era of AI-Enabled Cybercrime Criminal networks are utilizing AI to drastically increase both the volume and velocity of their operations. While investigators possess the tradecraft required to build high-stakes cases, the sheer scale of modern cybercrime has created a severe capacity gap that manual research and hiring alone cannot close. Recent findings from TRM Labs highlight the scale and speed of this evolving threat landscape: +40% Year-over-year surge in criminal use of AI according to TRM&#8217;s 2026 AI-in-Crime Adoption Index. $21B Losses reported to the FBI&#8217;s Internet Crime Complaint Center in 2025, roughly 5x the 2020 total. 93 New ransomware variants observed in 2025, representing a 94% increase from 2024. 51% Share of ransomware off-ramp volume concentrated across just five laundering services in 2025. Traditionally, cybercrime and nation-state investigations have been slowed down by fragmented intelligence : Evidence is scattered across silos: Threat reporting, domain and infrastructure records, infostealer compromises, and on-chain financial activity sit in separate tools, forcing analysts to spend valuable hours collecting data before they can even assess a campaign. Related attacks are investigated in isolation: Shared infrastructure, malware tooling, compromised credentials, and laundering services often link incidents across organizations, yet a single report rarely reveals the wider operation. Manual research delays operational action: Analysts repeatedly run searches across disconnected platforms and manually reconcile findings into briefings for law enforcement, defenders, and leadership. Understanding the Operation Behind the Attack with Hudson Rock &amp; Ardence Simply connecting a generic AI model to raw data does not provide the investigative tradecraft or audit trail needed for high-consequence environments. Featured prominently on TRM Labs&#8217; Cyber Attacks &amp; Cybercrime mission hub , Hudson Rock integrates directly into Ardence&#8217;s all-source intelligence architecture so investigators can ask questions in natural language and query across cyber, breach, identity, infrastructure, and blockchain sources in parallel. By pairing TRM Labs&#8217; blockchain and cross-source intelligence with Hudson Rock&#8217;s proprietary infostealer and cybercrime intelligence, analysts can seamlessly execute four core investigative workflows: 01 &middot; Research What do we know about this threat? Define threats, geographies, and timeframes in natural language. Ardence compares reported attacks, infrastructure, and campaign patterns across sources to generate source-backed summaries and highlight intelligence gaps. 02 &middot; Connect Which activity belongs to the same operation? Start with an incident report, domain, IP, hash, or handle. Enrich indicators against hosting, malware, infostealer infections, and breach records from Hudson Rock to unmask threat actor identities and shared infrastructure. 03 &middot; Trace What does the financial activity reveal? Pivot from a ransom wallet or stolen-funds transaction to trace on-chain transfers and identify receiving off-ramp venues, correlating blockchain trails directly with Hudson Rock&#8217;s technical and identity evidence. 04 &middot; Report What does your team need to know? Produce technical summaries, campaign assessments, or executive briefings that clearly separate observed facts from analytical assessments, keeping every finding linked to its underlying source material. Built for High-Stakes Scrutiny and Real-World Disruption As TRM Labs CEO and Co-Founder Esteban Casta&ntilde;o noted at launch, Ardence is engineered to take the most manual work off investigators&#8217; plates so the same team can work more cases without sacrificing rigor or precision: Connected Intelligence: The Ardence Data Network unites TRM intelligence with specialized cyber, dark web, identity, corporate, and financial intelligence, including Hudson Rock&#8217;s infostealer data, under a unified investigative workflow. Expert Tradecraft: Ardence applies repeatable analytic methods developed with experienced CTI and financial crime analysts to follow leads further than a team could by hand. Built for Scrutiny: Every finding stays strictly linked to its source material so reviewers can retrace the exact methods and human decisions behind a conclusion. Carried into Action: The unified investigation record directly supports drafting legal processes, asset-freeze requests, Disruption Sprints, and infrastructure takedowns, already helping global teams protect major international events, dismantle ransomware laundering pipelines, and take down thousands of scam sites. Through this partnership, Hudson Rock&#8217;s unmatched visibility into global info-stealer infections and cybercriminal ecosystems empowers Ardence users to connect compromised credentials, infected machines, and threat actor footprints directly to on-chain illicit finance, turning fragmented indicators into decisive disruption. 23 [Articles | InfoStealers] The 2026 Infostealer Index: How 34 Threat Intelligence Vendors Stack Up The 2026 Infostealer Index An independent, deep-dive analysis of…
The 2026 Infostealer Index An independent, deep-dive analysis of Hudson Rock&#8217;s benchmark threat intelligence evaluations. Ranking 34 vendors across 9 critical vectors. As infostealer malware continues to dominate the cyber threat landscape—fueling devastating breaches like the massive AI supply chain hack of 2026—the need for reliable infostealer intelligence has never been more critical. We recently analyzed Hudson Rock&#8217;s Infostealer Index , the industry&#8217;s premier benchmark for threat intelligence vendors. The 2026 index evaluates 34 different vendors across 9 rigorous criteria , breaking the market down into specialized categories. Below is our complete summary of the findings, including a deep dive into the methodology, the leaderboard, and how the top platforms compare. The Methodology To accurately rank capabilities, the index evaluates platforms based on a 100-point scale derived from nine crucial metrics: 01 Specialization Is the vendor inherently focused on infostealer malware, or is it a generalist tool incorporating feeds? 02 Machine Context The depth of information provided about the infected machine itself (IPs, OS versions, hardware specs). 03 Segmentation How effectively the raw data is parsed, structured, and made actionable for security operations. 04 Sessions Capabilities around tracking and validating active, authenticated sessions (cookies) extracted by malware. 05 Freshness The velocity at which newly compromised data is ingested and available to the platform&#8217;s users. 06 Research The quality of proprietary threat research, malware analysis, and reporting provided by the vendor. 07 Free Access Availability of community tools, free trials, employee notifications, or public search capabilities. 08 API The robustness, technical documentation, and ease of integration of the vendor&#8217;s enterprise API. 09 Accessibility Overall user experience (UX/UI), interface design, and minimization of operational friction. The Market Layout The 34 evaluated vendors fall into three distinct classifications based on their primary operational model: Infostealer Specialists (9 Vendors): Platforms explicitly built from the ground up to harvest, parse, and act on compromised machine data. (e.g., Hudson Rock, SpyCloud) CTI Platforms (19 Vendors): Broad Cyber Threat Intelligence platforms that include infostealer data as a module alongside dark web monitoring and OSINT. (e.g., Flare, Recorded Future) Lookup &#038; Search (6 Vendors): Search engines primarily focused on querying massive datasets of leaks, pastes, and stealer logs. (e.g., Have I Been Pwned, LeakRadar) Top 5 Vendors Analyzed #1 Hudson Rock Infostealer Specialist The undisputed leader. Perfect scores in Specialization, Machine Context, Segmentation, Research, and Free Access. 96.2 Overall Score Machine Context 10/10 Sessions 9/10 API 9/10 #2 Flare CTI Platform The highest-rated generalist CTI platform, featuring exceptional interface design and deep stealer-log coverage. 82.8 Overall Score Machine Context 9/10 Sessions 9/10 API 9/10 #3 SpyCloud Infostealer Specialist A powerhouse in session management and identity threat protection, slightly lagging in public free access tools. 81.3 Overall Score Machine Context 8/10 Sessions 10/10 API 9/10 #4 Breachsense Infostealer Specialist 70.4 API-first dark web and stealer-log monitor offering highly consistent backend capabilities. Sessions 8/10 #5 Lunar (Webz.io) Infostealer Specialist 70.4 Tied for 4th, Lunar provides incredible free access to compromised-credential monitoring. Hudson Rock dominates the market by providing unparalleled machine context and segmentation. However, depending on an organization&#8217;s specific operational needs, platforms like Flare (for CTI breadth) and SpyCloud (for hardcore Identity protection) stand as highly capable alternatives. As threat actors continue to bypass standard MFA via session cookie theft, leveraging vendors that score highly in Sessions and Machine Context is no longer optional for modern enterprises. The post The 2026 Infostealer Index: How 34 Threat Intelligence Vendors Stack Up appeared first on InfoStealers .

137 [DARKCLUB] Mèo Tài Lộc – Cách Chơi Slot Nổ Hũ Tại HITCLUB Mèo Tài Lộc trên nền tảng HITCLUB dành cho người chơi muốn tìm t…
Mèo Tài Lộc trên nền tảng HITCLUB dành cho người chơi muốn tìm trò nổ hũ dễ tiếp cận, có chủ đề may mắn rõ ràng và nhịp quay nhanh. Khi nắm được cơ chế slot, cách đọc biểu tượng, điều kiện kích hoạt thưởng và cách quản lý lượt quay, bạn sẽ chủ động hơn trước khi quyết định trải nghiệm trò chơi này trên nền tảng của chúng tôi. Mèo Tài Lộc Thuộc Nhóm Nào Trên Hitclub? Mèo Tài Lộc trong nhóm slot nổ hũ nhịp nhanh Tại đây, Mèo Tài Lộc được đặt trong nhóm slot Nổ hũ lấy cảm hứng từ mèo thần tài, hợp với người mới thích slot có nhịp chơi nhanh, hình ảnh dễ nhận biết và cách tham gia không quá phức tạp. Chủ Đề Mèo Thần Tài Trong Trò Chơi Điểm hút mắt đầu tiên của Mèo Tài Lộc nằm ở hình tượng mèo thần tài Maneki Neko, biểu tượng thường gắn với may mắn, chiêu tài và tài lộc trong văn hóa Á Đông, như câu chúc Cung Hỷ Phát Tài thường thấy trong dịp đầu năm. Khi đưa vào slot, hình ảnh mèo vẫy tay, đồng xu, sắc đỏ cùng ánh kim tạo cảm giác rộn ràng như không gian lễ hội nhỏ trên màn hình quay. Điểm cần phân biệt là chủ đề may mắn chỉ tạo cảm xúc và nhận diện hình ảnh. Kết quả mỗi lượt quay vẫn phụ thuộc vào cơ chế slot của trò chơi, không phải vì biểu tượng mèo thần tài xuất hiện mà xác suất thắng tự động cao hơn. Thông Tin Nhanh Về Trò Chơi Bảng dưới đây giúp bạn nắm nhanh tựa slot này thuộc nhóm nào, chơi ở đâu và cần chú ý điều gì trước khi quay. Hạng mục Thông tin Tên trò chơi Mèo Tài Lộc Thể loại Slot, nổ hũ, quay hũ theo lượt Nền tảng trải nghiệm Nền tảng Chủ đề chính Mèo may mắn, tài lộc, đồng xu, vật phẩm chiêu tài Cách chơi cơ bản Chọn mức cược, quay các cuộn biểu tượng, nhận kết quả theo tổ hợp Tính năng cần kiểm tra trong trò chơi Biểu tượng thưởng, wild, scatter, bonus, free spin, jackpot nếu giao diện hỗ trợ Đối tượng phù hợp Người chơi thích trò nhịp nhanh, dễ nhìn, muốn giải trí và quản lý quỹ chơi rõ ràng Cơ Chế Quay Hũ Cần Nắm Mèo Tài Lộc với cuộn quay và biểu tượng thưởng Muốn chơi tựa slot này thoải mái hơn, bạn nên hiểu cách một lượt quay được tạo ra trước khi quan tâm đến thưởng lớn. Phần cơ chế sẽ đi từ cuộn quay, dòng thắng đến các biểu tượng đặc biệt. Lượt Quay Tạo Thưởng Như Thế Nào? Một lượt quay tạo thưởng khi các biểu tượng dừng lại thành tổ hợp hợp lệ trên dòng thắng của trò chơi. Trong slot, các cột chuyển động được gọi là cuộn quay, còn dòng thắng là đường tính kết quả sau khi cuộn dừng. Người chơi chọn mức cược trước, sau đó hệ thống hiển thị tổ hợp biểu tượng cho lượt đó. Thưởng lượt quay = Mức cược × Hệ số/tổ hợp trả thưởng Công thức này chỉ là cách hiểu khái niệm. Nếu ba biểu tượng giống nhau nằm đúng trên một dòng thắng, trò chơi có thể tính thưởng theo bảng trả thưởng tương ứng. Mức cược là phần bạn chủ động chọn; kết quả tổ hợp là phần phát sinh sau lượt quay. Biểu Tượng Nào Đáng Chú Ý? Bạn có thể nắm phần trọng tâm trước khi tham gia: ngắm mục tiêu, tích điểm thưởng, theo dõi jackpot và chọn lối chơi phù hợp trong sảnh game cá HITCLUB. Trải Nghiệm Săn Hũ Rồng Thần Có Gì Hấp Dẫn? Bắn cá Săn Hũ Rồng Thần là trải nghiệm game cá online trong sảnh săn cá HITCLUB, nơi người chơi ngắm mục tiêu, tích điểm thưởng và theo dõi các cơ hội săn hũ theo cơ chế game. Khi boss xuất hiện trong Bắn cá Săn Hũ Rồng Thần, người chơi thường phải quan sát màn hình kỹ hơn, chọn hướng ngắm hợp lý và cân nhắc mức đạn đang dùng. Đây cũng là lúc ván chơi dễ chuyển từ nhịp đều sang nhịp tập trung, nên người mới không nên vội tăng đạn chỉ vì hiệu ứng đang nổi bật. Nếu quan sát thấy mục tiêu đi lệch tầm ngắm hoặc điểm giảm quá nhanh, nên chậm lại để đánh giá lại hướng ngắm. 147 [Data Breach] GTA 6 Leaker Demands $30M Meme Coin Market Cap to Release Full Game CyberLeek threatens to leak a playable GTA 6 build if its Solana…
CyberLeek threatens to leak a playable GTA 6 build if its Solana meme coin hits a $30 million market cap, but the build remains unverified. CyberLeek, the anonymous figure behind this summer’s wave of GTA 6 leaks , just raised the stakes considerably. After dropping 25 minutes of gameplay footage earlier last week, including nudity and dev menu access, they’re now threatening to release a complete, playable build of the game, start to finish. &#8220;A GTA 6 leaker has reportedly threatened to put out a complete, playable build of the game, start to finish.&#8221; reads Eurogamer.net . &#8220;The threat was posted on CyberLeek&#8217;s website after the leaker shared 25 minutes of footage from the game earlier this week.&#8221; CyberLeek posted the threat on its own website. The demand is simple: if its cryptocurrency reaches a $30 million market value, the leaker will release the playable build of GTA 6, including the full story mode, from start to finish, with the ending credits. CyberLeek says Take-Two can avoid the leak only by providing physical copies of the game and issuing a public apology. This sounds more like a challenge than a serious negotiation. &#8220;Here&#8217;s the deal, nice and clean,&#8221; the leaker states. &#8220;CyberLeek hits 30 million marketcap, the build goes out — full story mode, played to the end, credits and all. Don&#8217;t believe it has full story mode? Ask for what you want to see, and it gets provided. Take-Two gets one way out: physical disks and a public apology. They can take it at any moment. Or they can gamble that CL is bluffing. Everyone knows which one they&#8217;ll pick.&#8221; This is not CyberLeek’s first leak. Rockstar previously said the situation was painful and that this was not how it wanted fans to see the game after years of waiting. Take-Two, Rockstar’s parent company, has been trying to identify the leaker and even sought a court order requiring Discord to share information about their identity. The company is actively trying to stop the leaks rather than waiting for the situation to pass. Take-Two, Rockstar’s parent company, has been actively hunting for the leaker and tried subpoenaing Discord for identifying information, so this isn’t a studio sitting back and hoping it blows over. The efforts to stop the leaks have not worked so far. CyberLeek went silent for a while, then returned with new footage showing Jason fully naked and Lucia topless. Eurogamer reported that the video appears to be real and carries CyberLeek’s usual watermark. CyberLeek did more than leak game footage. It also launched a Solana meme coin and uses the videos to promote it. Reports said the coin reached a market value of $25 million in late August, with more than $36 million in trading volume in a single day. Its value then fell sharply after Rockstar released official gameplay footage on Netflix and interest in the leaks declined. The new $30 million target is important because reaching it could trigger the release of a playable version of GTA 6 before the official launch. Whether CyberLeek genuinely has a full playable build worth leaking is still unverified. &#8220;The knowledge that a playable build of GTA 6 might be online has unsurprisingly encouraged hackers to lure curious users searching for it on unfamiliar websites.&#8221; states TechSpot . &#8220;Files labeled as the build have recently appeared on torrent websites, but these are certainly malicious. There is no evidence that CyberLeek or anyone else has, or even can, release an early version of the game.&#8221; Fake downloads claiming to be the leaked build have already started circulating on torrent and piracy sites, several reportedly carrying malware, so anyone tempted to go looking is more likely to get infected than to get an early copy of GTA 6. Take-Two has already lost close to $2.8 billion in market value over a 48 hour stretch tied to this leak saga, which tells you how seriously the market is taking the threat, even unverified. GTA 6 is still set for a November 19 release on PlayStation 5 and Xbox Series X/S, and neither Rockstar nor Take-Two has commented on this latest ultimatum. Extortion tied to a crypto pump scheme is a genuinely new flavor of leak drama, and it probably won’t be the last time someone tries combining the two. 148 [Data Breach] Burger King Japan Data Breach Impacted 11 Million Customers Burger King Japan disclosed a breach exposing 11.18 million app …
Burger King Japan disclosed a breach exposing 11.18 million app users&#8217; personal data after attackers accessed its membership system in early October. Burger King Japan has disclosed a data breach that exposed the personal information of approximately 11.18 million official app members. Burger King Japan, the company operating the fast-food chain in the country, said attackers accessed the system managing customer membership information between October 2 and October 7, 2026. The company disclosed the incident on October 15, after discovering the data leak two days earlier. In its official notice published on October 15, the company confirmed the unauthorized access and apologized to affected customers. The announcement doesn&#8217;t identify the attackers or explain how they gained access. BK Japan says it&#8217;s working with external security specialists to determine the cause. “We have confirmed that customer membership information from the official app has been leaked externally following unauthorized access by a third party.” reads the data breach notification. The compromised data includes membership IDs, email addresses, names in Japanese characters and kana, phone numbers, dates of birth, gender, prefecture of residence, and membership registration type. The exposed information also indicates whether a customer has a mobile ordering history and whether their email address has been verified. These details can be useful to criminals even without payment data or passwords. Attackers can use them to craft convincing phishing emails, text messages, or phone calls that refer to a customer&#8217;s real identity and relationship with Burger King. A message that includes a person&#8217;s name, phone number, and other accurate details may look far more credible than a generic scam. Burger King Japan confirmed it does not store credit card numbers and found no evidence that passwords were exposed, with no confirmed cases of fraud so far. However, these factors do not completely rule out the risk of future misuse. According to the company, unauthorized access took place from October 2 through October 7, and the leak was confirmed on October 13. BK Japan says it blocked access from the source of the unauthorized communications as soon as it became aware of the incident. The company has also introduced temporary countermeasures, established an incident response team, and reported the breach to Japan&#8217;s Personal Information Protection Commission. It says permanent security improvements, further investigation with external specialists, and email notifications to affected customers are ongoing. “At present, we have confirmed that the unauthorized access was carried out by a third party.” continues the notification. The company has not yet disclosed the precise circumstances that made the intrusion possible, and the investigation is continuing. Until more details emerge, it&#8217;s too early to draw conclusions about the initial access vector or attribute the attack to a particular threat actor. Large-scale data breaches create long-lasting risks for follow-on attacks, as criminals can use stolen details for phishing, impersonation, and highly convincing scams. BK Japan urges customers to stay vigilant against fraudulent emails, texts, and calls, warning them never to click unverified links or share sensitive credentials. The core danger is that attackers no longer need network access to cause harm; they can leverage existing stolen data to target customers directly through social engineering. “Please do not access URLs, open attachments, or enter membership information or passwords if you receive a suspicious communication.” concludes the report. Я говорю о том, что текст промта получается раз в 10 больше чем программный код и результат ИИ непредсказуем. Ты на промт тратишь больше времени и, а после еще и рефакторишь код, который не сам писал это вообще задница. Когда ты сам пишешь код, то называешь переменные понятными именами cancel_button apply_button... ИИ тебе понаришет button_1 button_2 button_3 - вот сиди и разбирай потом что он там понаписал. Проще самому написать 150 строк готового отрефакторенного программного кода за час, чем целый день писать промт на 1500 и получить 500 строк неведомого кода.

giodentalspa.com Gio Dental & Med Spa private dental and aesthetic clinic founded around 2006 in Ciudad Juárez, Mexico, targeting patients from the US border region of El Paso and Las Cruces. The practice has about 6 employees and estimated annual revenue of roughly 430000 dollars, making it a small self-owned business with no external funding. Its business model is built on cross-border dental tourism, accepting American HMO and PPO insurance and transporting patients from El Paso directly to the clinic. Core services are dental implants, orthodontics, endodontics, veneers and oral surgery, plus a med spa division with botox, fillers and physiotherapy. The clinical founder and director is Dr. Mauro D. Álvarez, an implantology specialist, working with a team of about five additional dentists. The company is positioned on price: complex dental procedures cost a fraction of US rates while offering full-service treatment and aftercare for American patients.

271 [ipbmafia] Чат для общения. Все равно у меня для промпт инженеров плохие новости. В при пост…
Все равно у меня для промпт инженеров плохие новости. В при построении точного ТЗ очень важно понимать принципы проектирования, алгоритмы, алгоритмическую сложность, и структуры данных. Та же самая сортировка имеет кучу методов, которые применяются в разных ситуациях. Промпт инженер обязан знать и понимать все типы сортировок, чтобы требовать их применения у LLM. Промпт инженер обязан знать и понимать принцип работы деревьев, графов, детерминированных конечных автоматов, чтобы знать где и в какой момент требовать их применения у LLM. Так же важно понимать где наивный способ с квадратичной сложностью может оказаться быстрее высокоскоростных O(1) структур. Знание языка и синтаксиса это не самое важное умение. В большинстве случаев даже вообще не важно. Важно понимать архитектуру и уметь проектировать высоко нагруженные системы. Поэтому высокую ценность будут иметь промпт инженеры, которые понимают, что за ахинею я выше написал.

wellingtoncollege.ac.th Wellington College Bangkok is a British international school for ages 2 to 18, opened in 2018 as part of Wellington College UK, with around 1100 students on a 28 acre campus in eastern Bangkok. The curriculum runs from the English National Curriculum to IGCSE and A Levels, with the first graduating class in 2026. Fees range from about 639000 baht per year in pre nursery up to 1283500 baht in Year 12, plus a 225000 baht registration fee and a 175000 baht refundable deposit. Results are elite level with 80 percent A star or A at IGCSE and every graduate of 2026 admitted to their first choice university including Stanford, Yale and Imperial College. Admissions are selective with CAT4 testing and interviews, and strong English is required from Year 10. The school is famous for its Olympic pool, FIFA standard pitch, 600 seat theatre, wellbeing curriculum and scholarships in music, swimming, golf and academics.

275 [THE GENTLEMEN] AirQ airq-ad.com AirQ UAE drone manufacturer founded in 2021 in Masda…
airq-ad.com AirQ UAE drone manufacturer founded in 2021 in Masdar City, Abu Dhabi, producing unmanned aerial systems fully in-house. It was started by Croatian entrepreneurs Dino Dedić as CEO and Igor Pongrac as CTO, who previously worked at Rimac Automobili, and has grown from 6 people to about 100 engineers. The flagship product is the Harara modular VTOL drone, carrying 6 kg of payload and flying up to 7 hours or 600 km in hybrid mode, with over 1000 test flights completed. Other products include the StingJet reusable jet target drone for air defense training and the world's first fully electric UAV launcher. The company is backed by Abu Dhabi institutions such as Tawazun Council and Masdar City Free Zone and runs real operations like medical blood sample delivery between hospitals. Its main strengths are full in-house composite production and dual defense plus commercial markets, while the main risks are its young age, ongoing aviation certification and dependence on UAE defense.
 ransomlook 10-16 22:30
276 [ipbmafia] Чат для общения. Исторически знание алгоритмов и структур данных было обязательны…
Исторически знание алгоритмов и структур данных было обязательным условием разработки. Машина понимала только формальный код, а перевод цели в реализацию требовал ручного труда. Сортировки, деревья, графы, оценка сложности - всё это было инструментом трансляции намерения в рабочее решение. С развитием LLM этот слой перестал быть уникальным навыком человека. Модель воспроизводит выбор между алгоритмами и структурами точнее среднего разработчика, потому что не имеет пробелов в базе и не зависит от усталости. Знание алгоритмов из обязательного условия превратилось в опциональный инструмент верификации. Уровень 4 - работа с намерением - не требует знания B-tree и конечных автоматов. Требуется умение формулировать цель, задавать ограничения и проверять результат. LLM закрывает разрыв между намерением и реализацией, и этот разрыв сокращается по мере роста моделей. Постановщик задачи всегда стоял выше исполнителя - не по статусу, а по вкладу: кто определяет "что" и "зачем"», задаёт рамку для тех, кто делает "как". Раньше "как" требовало отдельного человека, теперь машина переводит намерение в реализацию сама, исполнительский слой автоматизировался и ценность сместилась к постановке задачи, а знание алгоритмов осталось полезным, но перестало быть обязательным условием результата. Вот и коробит тебя, ты же учился и думал, что исполнитель - это навсегда передовой востребованный уровень. А история повернулась к тебе не самым приятным местом.

278 [ipbmafia] Чат для общения. Программисты всегда будут более лучшими промпт инженерами нежели…
Программисты всегда будут более лучшими промпт инженерами нежели люди которые возомнили себя таковыми. Человек, который понимает архитектуру, алгоритмы и структуры, всегда будет впереди того кто ничего не знает и знать не хочет. Даже когда ИИ будет напрямую генерировать бинарники, минуя генерацию исходного кода. Люди со знанием архитектур, алгоритмов и структур всегда будут впереди. Если вы не в курсе, то ИИ вообще не нужен исходный код. Он может сразу компилировать промпты. Но это не снимает с человека обязанность понимать как это всё работает. Как в старой прговорке. Тот кто читает книги всегда будет умнее того кто смотрит телевизор.
